Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jan Błachowicz]] and [[Aleksandar Rakić]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN 36]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/1/27/22905404/jan-blachowicz-vs-aleksandar-rakic-scratched-from-ufc-columbus|title=Jan Blachowicz vs. Aleksandar [[Chris Daukaus]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/02/ufc-columbus-chris-daukaus-vs-curtis-blaydes|title=Curtis Blaydes vs. Chris Daukuas to serve as new UFC Reported payout ==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the Ohio Athletic Commission. The amounts do not include sponsor money, discretionary bonuses, viewership points or additional earnings. The total disclosed payout for the event was $1,717,000.{{Cite web|date=2022-03-29|title=Curtis Blaydes, Neil Magny lead all salaries at UFC Columbus|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/3/29/23001853/curtis-blaydes-neil-magny-lead-all-salaries-at-ufc-columbus |access-date=2022-03-30|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin}}\n\n* '''Curtis Blaydes:''' $262,000 (includes $131,000 win bonus) def. Chris Daukaus: $80,000\n* '''Alexa Grasso:''' $86,000 (includes $43,000 win bonus) def. Joanne Wood: $70,000\n* '''Kai Kara-France:''' $102,000 (includes $51,000 win bonus) def. Askar Askarov: $26,000\n* '''Bryan Barberena:''' $122,000 (includes $61,000 win bonus) def. Matt Brown: $105,000\n* '''Manon Fiorot:''' $80,000 (includes $40,000 win bonus) def. Jennifer Maia: $60,000\n* '''Neil Magny:''' $230,000 (includes $115,000 win bonus) def. Max Griffin: $53,000\n* '''Marc Diakiese:''' $66,000 (includes $33,000 win bonus) def. Viachaslav Borshchev: $12,000\n* '''Sara McMann:''' $88,000 (includes $44,000 win bonus) def. Karol Rosa: $28,000\n* '''Chris Gutiérrez:''' $72,000 (includes $36,000 win bonus) def. Batgerel Danaa: $28,000\n* '''Matheus Nicolau:''' $44,000 (includes $22,000 win bonus) def. David Dvořák: $35,000\n* '''Aliaskhab Khizriev:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. [[UFC on ESPN: Luque vs. Muhammad 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 273: Volkanovski vs. The Korean Zombie''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 9, 2022, at [[VyStar Veterans Memorial Arena]] in [[Jacksonville, Florida]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/02/ufc-273-news-jacksonville-florida-volkanovski-vs-jung-yan-sterling-2|title=UFC 273 heads back to Jacksonville with two title fights atop the bill|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-02-04|access-date=2022-02-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was initially linked to the [[Barclays Center]] in [[Brooklyn, New York]].{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-12-30|title=UFC 273 targeted for Barclays Center in Brooklyn on April 9|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/12/30/22860694/ufc-273-targeted-for-barclays-center-in-brooklyn-on-april-9|access-date=2021-12-30|publisher=mmafighting.com}} However, the promotion opted to move it to [[VyStar Veterans Memorial Arena]] in [[Jacksonville, Florida]].\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former title challenger [[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/1/11/22872809/alexander-volkanovski-vs-the-korean-zombie-aljamain-sterling-vs-petr-yan-2-set-to-headline-ufc-273|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs. The Korean Zombie, Aljamain Sterling vs. Petr Yan 2 set to headline UFC 273|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-01-11|access-date=2021-01-11}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] title unification rematch between current champion [[Aljamain Sterling]] and former champion/current interim title holder [[Petr Yan]] took place at this event. The pairing previously met at [[UFC 259]], with Sterling winning the title by disqualification (intentional illegal knee strike) in the fourth round, becoming the first fighter to win a UFC title by disqualification.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-259-results-highlights-aljamain-sterling-earns-135-pound-title-after-petr-yans-illegal-knee-leads-to-dq/|title=UFC 259 results: Aljamain Sterling earns 135-pound title after Petr Yan's illegal knee leads to DQ|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Dan Doherty|date=2021-01-11|access-date=2022-01-11}} The rematch was initially expected to take place at [[UFC 267]], however Sterling withdrew from the contest due to lingering neck issues and an interim title bout between Yan and [[Cory Sandhagen]] was set.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/25/22693315/aljamain-sterling-out-of-ufc-267-title-fight-vs-petr-yan|title=Aljamain Sterling out of UFC 267 title fight vs. Petr Yan|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2021-09-25|access-date=2021-01-11}} The bout was then scheduled for [[UFC 272]] before being moved to this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/1/5/22869471/alexander-volkanovski-vs-max-holloway-3-aljamain-sterling-vs-petr-yan-2-booked-for-ufc-272|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs. Max Holloway 3, Aljamain Sterling vs. Petr Yan 2 booked for UFC 272|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-01-05|access-date=2022-01-11|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]] and [[Marcin Tybura]] was initially scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Makhachev vs. Green]].{{Cite web|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-01-09|title=Heavyweights Jairzinho Rozenstruik, Marcin Tybura to meet at Feb. 26 UFC event|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Heavyweights-Jairzinho-Rozenstruik-Marcin-Tybura-to-Meet-at-Feb-26-UFC-Event-184057|access-date=2022-01-09|publisher=sherdog.com}} However, in mid January it was announced the bout was moved to this card.{{Cite web|author=Jesse Holland|date=2022-01-13|title=Jairzinho Rozenstruik vs Marcin Tybura rebooked for UFC 273 on April 9|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/1/13/22882164/jairzinho-rozenstruik-vs-marcin-tybura-rebooked-ufc-273-april-9-mma|access-date=2022-01-14|publisher=mmamania.com}} In turn, the bout was pulled from the card after Tybura withdrew due to an undisclosed illness.{{Cite Fighting Championship|access-date=2022-02-05}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/4/10/23018702/ufc-273-post-fight-bonuses-khamzat-chimaev-vs-gilbert-burns-wins-fight-of-the-night|title=UFC 273 post-fight bonuses: Khamzat Chimaev vs. Gilbert Burns wins 'Fight of the Night'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2022-04-10|access-date=2022-04-10}}\n\n* '''Fight of the Night: Khamzat Chimaev vs. Gilbert Burns'''\n* '''Performance of the Night: Alexander Volkanovski''' and '''Alexey Oleynik'''\n\nWhile the UFC usually gives out four bonus awards for each event, three \"Fan Bonus of the Night\" will be awarded by [[Crypto.com]], as part of their UFC sponsorship incentive fight kits deal, for every pay-per-view event starting with UFC 273.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/4/7/23014643/ufc-to-debut-crypto-com-bitcoin-fan-bonus-of-the-night-for-fighters-at-ufc-273|title=UFC to debut Crypto.com Bitcoin 'Fan Bonus of the Night' for fighters at UFC 273|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-04-07|access-date=2022-04-07}} Viewers could vote up to three times per pay-per-view on Crypto.com/FanBonus, starting from the opening of the PPV preliminary card and ending an hour after the conclusion of the main card. The bonuses are paid in [[bitcoin]] in US dollars ranging from US$30,000 for first place, US$20,000 for second place, and US$10,000 for third place.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/04/ufc-crypto-fan-bonus-of-the-night-bitcoin|title=UFC adds Fan Bonus of the Night for fighters to be paid in bitcoin|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-04-07|access-date=2022-04-07}} The winners of the inaugural awards were:\n\n* '''First Place:''' Khamzat Chimaev\n* '''Second Place:''' headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-01-25|title=Vicente Luque vs. Belal Muhammad 2 headlines UFC event on April undisclosed reasons and replaced by Brandon Jenkins.{{cite to headline UFC Fight Night event on April 30|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells, Nolan King, and Mike Bohn|date=2021-03-10|access-date=2021-03-12}} At the weigh-ins, Font weighed to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/ufc-fight-night-198-jessica-penne-out-sam-hughes-vs-luana-pinheiro|title=With Jessica Penne out, Sam Hughes steps in It was the city's first event since [[UFC on ESPN: Ngannou vs. Velasquez]] in February 2019.\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Glover Teixeira]] and former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Light Heavyweight Championship|Rizin Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jiří Procházka]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/33123401/sources-glover-teixeira-jiri-prochazka-light-heavyweight-title-set-ufc-274-may|title=Sources: Glover Teixeira-Jiri Prochazka light heavyweight title bout set for UFC 274 in May|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2022-01-22|access-date=2022-01-22}} However, the bout was pushed back to [[UFC 275]] for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/2/27/22953018/glover-teixeira-vs-jiri-prochazka-championship-fight-at-ufc-274-rescheduled-for-ufc-275|title=Glover Teixeira vs. Jiri Prochazka championship fight at UFC 274 rescheduled for UFC 275|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-02-27|access-date=2022-02-27}}\n\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Charles Oliveira]] and former interim champion [[Justin Gaethje]] (also former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]]) headlined the event.{{Cite web|date=2022-01-26|title=Justin Gaethje set to challenge Charles Oliveira for lightweight title at UFC 274|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-news-justin-gaethje-set-to-challenge-charles-oliveira-for-lightweight-title-at-ufc-274|access-date=2022-01-27|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Danny Segura}} At the weigh-ins, Oliveira weighed in at 155.5 pounds, half a pound over the title limit. As a result, upon commencement of the fight, Oliveira was stripped of the title and only Gaethje was eligible to win it.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/ufc-274-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-noon-et|title=UFC 274 weigh-in results: Charles Oliveira misses weight, stripped of title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-05-06|access-date=2022-05-06}} \n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] rematch between current two-time champion [[Rose Namajunas]] and former champion (also former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]]) [[Carla Esparza]] took place at the event.{{Cite Crowned Finale]] for the inaugural championship, which Esparza won via third round submission.{{Cite Vera]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-news-donald-cerrone-vs-joe-lauzon-cowboy-april-30|title=UFC targeting Donald Cerrone vs. Joe Lauzon for April 30 7|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-02-15|access-date=2022-02-15}} In turn, the bout was cancelled on the day of the event when Cerrone suffered from food poisoning.{{Cite Chiasson]].\n\nIt was announced during the event's broadcast that former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight]] and [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|Heavyweight Champion]] [[Daniel Championship]] that took place on July 2, 2022, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada|Paradise]], [[Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Adam Guillen Jr.|date=2022-01-24|access-date=2022-01-26}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was held during the UFC's 10th annual International Fight Week. The 2022 [[UFC Hall of Fame]] induction ceremony also took place during the week.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/01/23/ufc-276-2022-hall-fame-ceremony-highlight-international-fight-week-july/|title=UFC 276, 2022 Hall of Fame ceremony highlight International Fight Week in Cannonier]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/5/7/23054022/israel-adesanya-vs-jared-cannonier-booked-for-ufc-276-main-event|title=Israel Adesanya vs. Jared Cannonier Martin|date=2022-05-08|access-date=2022-05-08|archive-date=2023-09-25|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230925161714/https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2022/5/7/23054022/israel-adesanya-vs-jared-cannonier-booked-for-ufc-276-main-event|url-status=dead}}\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] trilogy bout between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former champion [[Max Holloway]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/5/5/23058491/alexander-volkanovski-vs-max-holloway-3-targeted-for-ufc-276|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs. Max Holloway 3 targeted for UFC title.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/12/ufc-245-results-alexander-volkanovski-dethrones-max-holloway-captures-ufc-featherweight-belt|title=UFC 245 results: Alexander Volkanovski dethrones Max Holloway, captures UFC featherweight belt|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2019-12-15|access-date=2022-01-05}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC 251]], with Volkanovski successfully defending the title via split decision. They were originally expected to meet for the third time at [[UFC 272]], but Holloway withdrew due to an aggravated previous injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-272-max-holloway-injured-out-vs-alexander-volkanovski-trilogy-title-fight|title=Max Holloway injury takes him out of UFC 272 trilogy fight vs. '''''UFC Vegas 55''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 21, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[Ketlen Vieira]] headlined the event.\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ben Rothwell]] and former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alexander Gustafsson]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|author=Jed Meshew|date=2022-02-02|title=Alexander Gustafsson returns to the heavyweight division, faces Ben Rothwell at May 21 UFC event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/2/2/22912588/alexander-gustafsson-returns-to-the-heavyweight-division-faces-ben-rothwell-at-may-21-ufc-event|access-date=2022-02-01|publisher=mmafighting.com}} Blaydes vs. Aspinall]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/4/27/23044979/ufc-alexander-gustafsson-returns-light-heavyweight-fights-nikita-krylov-july-23-mma|title=Alexander Gustafsson returns [[Jailton Almeida]] in a light heavyweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/ufc/2022/ufc-toptalent-almeida-vecht-tegen-grishin-op-21-mei_sto8843870/story.shtml|title=Top talent Almeida fights Grishin on May 21|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2022-03-14|access-date=2022-03-14}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Grishin pulled out due to undisclosed reasons in late April. Almeida decided to move up to heavyweight and faced [[Parker Porter]] instead.{{Cite MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com.br/news/rafael-dos-anjos-rafael-fiziev-luta-principal-ufc-fight-night-9-de-julho|title=Rafael dos Anjos and Rafael Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[Rafael Fiziev]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/ufc/2022/ufc-derde-poging-voor-dos-anjos-vs.-fiziev-ditmaal-als-main-event-op-9-juli_sto8912634/story.shtml|title=UFC third attempt for dos Anjos vs. Fiziev, this time as the main event on July 9th|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2022-05-05|access-date=2022-05-06}} {{in lang|nl}} They were previously scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Walker vs. Hill]], but the bout was postponed to [[UFC 272]] due to visa issues with Fiziev.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/2/11/22929558/rafael-dos-anjos-vs-rafael-fiziev-off-ufc-vegas-48-rebooked-for-ufc-272-co-main-event-mma|title=Rafael dos Anjos vs Rafael Fiziev off UFC Vegas 48, positive for COVID-19]] a week before that event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/2/28/22955195/rafael-fiziev-out-of-ufc-272-fight-against-rafael-dos-anjos-due-to-covid-19 |title=Rafael Dern vs. Rodriguez]], but Winn pulled out both times due to injuries.{{Cite event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 16, 2022, at [[UBS Arena]] in [[Elmont, New York]] in the [[United States]].{{cite web|url=https://greaterlongisland.com/ufc-fight-night-comes-to-elmonts-ubs-arena-on-july-16/|title=UFC Fight Night comes to Elmont's UBS Arena on July 16|publisher=greaterlongisland.com|author=Brian Harmon|date=2022-05-26|access-date=2022-05-26}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-on-abc-3-ortega-vs-rodriguez-main-card-to-air|title=UFC announces ABC will air Brian Ortega vs. Yair Rodriguez main card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-06-01|access-date=2022-06-01}}\n\n==Background==\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Brian Ortega]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/ufc-news-brian-ortega-vs-yair-rodriguez-july-16-main-event|title=UFC books Brian Ortega vs. Yair Rodriguez as July 16 main event on Long Island|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-05-26|access-date=2022-05-26}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Askar Askarov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/2022/04/askar-askarov-vs-alex-perez/|title=Askar Askarov vs. Alex Perez set for July 16 UFC Fight Night|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Curtis Calhoun|date=2022-04-13|access-date=2022-04-14}} The pair was previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC on ESPN: Hall vs. Strickland]] in July 2021, but Askarov pulled out citing injury.{{cite web|url=https://espndeportes.espn.com/artes-marciales-mixtas/nota/_/id/8880873/askar-askarov-alex-perez-matt-schnell-brandon-moreno-ufc|title=Askar Askarov breaks his hand and will miss his fight against Alex Pérez; Matt Schnell, the replacement|publisher=espndeportes.espn.com|author=Carlos Contreras Legaspi|date=2021-07-07|access-date=2021-07-08}} {{in lang|es}} Askarov pulled out once again in early June due to undisclosed reasons and Perez was rescheduled against [[Alexandre Pantoja]] at [[UFC 277]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-277-alexandre-pantoja-vs-alex-perez-booked|title=Alexandre Pantoja vs. Alex Perez in the works for UFC 277 on July 30|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-06-09|access-date=2022-06-09}}\n\nA women’s strawweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger and inaugural [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Jessica Penne]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Brianna Fortino]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-may-2-8-2022|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (May 2-8)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-05-09|access-date=2022-06-02}} However, Fortino withdrew in early June for unknown reasons and was replaced by another former Invicta FC Strawweight Champion in [[Emily Ducote]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-news-emily-ducote-invicta-fc-champ-signs-vs-jessica-penne-long-island|title=Invicta FC champion Emily Ducote signs with UFC, fights Jessica Penne on Long Island|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-06-02|access-date=2022-06-02}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miesha Tate]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] (also former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger) [[Lauren Murphy]] was originally expected for [[UFC on ESPN: Błachowicz vs. Rakić]] but was then shifted to [[UFC 276]] for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/miesha-tate-vs-lauren-murphy-booked-ufc-273-april|title=Miesha Tate vs. Lauren Murphy booked for May|publisher=fightful.com|author=Evan Wheeler|date=2022-01-10|access-date=2022-06-27}}{{Cite 276|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2022-03-16|access-date=2022-06-27}} In turn, a week before that event, Murphy pulled out after she [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/6/26/23183436/lauren-murphy-vs-miesha-tate-scratched-from-ufc-276-lineup|title=Lauren Murphy vs. Miesha Tate scratched from UFC web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/6/27/23183472/miesha-tate-vs-lauren-murphy-shifts-to-ufc-long-island|title=Miesha Tate vs. Lauren Murphy shifts to UFC Long Island|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-06-27|access-date=2022-06-27}}\n\nA couple of featherweight bouts featuring [[Bill Algeo]] vs. [[Billy Quarantillo]] and [[Herbert Burns]] vs. Khusein Askhabov were scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/ufc-news-bill-algeo-vs-billy-quarantillo-july-16|title=Battle of the Bills: UFC books Bill against each other.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/8/23200093/bill-algeo-herbert-burns-ufc-long-island-billy-quarantillo-khusein-askhabov-out|title=Bill Algeo vs. Herbert Burns now Nurmagomedov]] were expected to meet in a welterweight contest.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/17/phil-rowe-vs-abubakar-nurmagomedov-added-ufc-long-island/|title=Phil Rowe vs. Abubakar Nurmagomedov added to UFC Long Island|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-17|access-date=2022-07-12}} However, the pairing was cancelled due to complications on both sides. Rowe has been forced to withdraw due to an injury, while Nurmagomedov pulled out due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues which restricted his travel.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/12/23205665/phil-rowe-vs-abubakar-nurmagomedov-off-ufc-on-abc-3|title=Phil Rowe vs. Abubakar Nurmagomedov off UFC on ABC 3|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. vs. Cruz]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Santos vs. Hill''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 40''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 59''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 6, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/6/15/23169908/latest-ufc-vegas-59-fight-card-espn2-lineup-santos-vs-hill-aug-6-apex-mma|title=Latest UFC Vegas 59 fight card, ESPN2 lineup for ‘Santos vs. Hill’ on Aug. 6|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2022-06-15|access-date=2022-06-27}}\n\n==Background==\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight]] title challenger [[Thiago Santos]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/ufc-news-jamahal-hill-vs-thiago-santos-main-event-august-6|title=UFC books Thiago Santos vs. Jamahal Hill for Aug. 6 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2022-05-04|access-date=2022-05-04}}\n\nThe heavyweight and women's flyweight finals of [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Peña vs. Team Nunes]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-tuf-30-finale-heavyweights-flyweights-amanda-nunes-vs-julianna-pena|title=TUF 30 heavyweight and women's flyweight finals set for UFC on ESPN 40|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-07-19|access-date=2021-07-19}}\n\nFormer [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Women's flyweight championship|KSW Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Ariane Lipski]] and [[Priscila Cachoeira]] were expected to meet in a flyweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/3/23193630/ariane-lipski-vs-priscila-cachoeira-set-for-ufc-vegas-59|title=Ariane Lipski vs. Priscila Cachoeira set for UFC Vegas 59|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-07-03|access-date=2022-07-03}} They were previously scheduled to fight at [[UFC Fight Night: Błachowicz vs. Jacaré]] in November 2019, but Cachoeira withdrew after being suspended for the use of a diuretic.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/11/6/20952536/veronica-macedo-steps-in-to-fight-ariane-lipski-at-ufc-sao-paulo|title=Veronica Macedo steps in to fight Ariane Lipski at UFC Sao Paulo|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2019-11-06|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2019-11-07}} At the weigh-ins, Lipski weighed in at 128.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. The bout was originally expected to proceed at catchweight with Lipski fined 20% of her purse.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-espn-40-thiago-santos-jamahal-hill-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ESPN 40 weigh-in results: Santos, Hill, 'TUF' finalists on weight, but Lipski misses mark|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-08-05|access-date=2022-08-05}} However, the fight was removed from the card on the day of the event due to Lipski not being medically cleared, and was postponed one week to [[UFC on ESPN: Vera vs. Cruz]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-on-espn-40-news-ariane-lipski-priscila-cachoeira-bout-rescheduled-san-diego|title=Ariane Lipski not medically cleared, UFC on ESPN 40 bout vs. Priscila Cachoeira rescheduled|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-08-06|access-date=2022-08-06}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Nate Landwehr]] and [[Zubaira Tukhugov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/07/13/nate-landwehr-vs-zubaira-tukhugov-added-august-6-ufc-event/|title=Nate Landwehr vs. Zubaira Tukhugov Added To August 6 UFC Event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin|date=2022-07-13|access-date=2022-07-24}} However, Tukhugov pulled out of the fight due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues which restricted his travel and was replaced by David Onama at [[UFC on ESPN: Vera vs. Cruz]] a week later.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/07/21/david-onama-replaces-zubaira-tukhugov-nate-landwehr-ufc-san-diego/|title=David Onama replaces Zubaira Tukhugov against Nate Landwehr, fight moves to UFC San Diego|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-21|access-date=2022-07-24}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Jason Witt]] and Josh Quinlan was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/14/jason-witt-vs-josh-quinlan-booked-ufcs-august-6-card/|title=Jason Witt welcomes Josh Quinlan to the UFC on August 6|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-14|access-date=2022-08-08}} However, it was pushed back one week to UFC on ESPN: Vera vs. Cruz after Quinlan was pulled for an atypical drug finding: long-term metabolite (or M3 metabolite) of the steroid [[dehydrochloromethyltestosterone]] (DHCMT) in his urine sample.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/6/23295090/josh-quinlan-not-cleared-for-ufc-vegas-59-after-steroid-metabolite-turns-up-in-drug-test|title=Josh Quinlan not cleared for UFC Vegas 59 after Championship]] rematch between current champion (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] bantamweight winner) [[Julianna Peña]] and former champion (also then [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]]) [[Amanda Nunes]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2022/5/21/23136267/julianna-pena-vs-amanda-nunes-2-announced-as-ufc-277-main-event|title=Julianna Pena vs. Amanda Nunes 2 announced as UFC 277 main The pairing previously met at [[UFC 269]] in last December, where Peña captured the title by submission in the second round in a major upset.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-269-results-highlights-julianna-pena-scores-shocking-upset-of-amanda-nunes-to-claim-title/amp/|title=UFC 269 results, highlights: Julianna Pena scores shocking upset of Amanda Nunes to claim title|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|date=2021-12-21|access-date=2022-05-21}} They were also the head coaches for the [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Peña vs. Team Nunes|30th season]] of [[The Ultimate Fighter]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/02/ufc-news-the-ultimate-fighter-30-tuf-julianna-pena-vs-amanda-nunes-2|title='The Ultimate Fighter' Season 30 to be coached by Julianna Pena and Amanda Nunes; rematch to follow|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-02-04|access-date=2022-02-05}} This was the seventh time in UFC history that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[UFC 94]], [[UFC 205]], [[UFC 226]], [[UFC 232]], [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Dillashaw]] and [[UFC 259]].\n\nAn interim [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between former champion [[Brandon Moreno]] and [[Kai Kara-France]] took place at the event.{{Cite Lewis\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn February 3, 2023, it was announced that the [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) suspended [[Hamdy Abdelwahab]] for two years after he tested positive twice for anabolic agent [[methenolone]] and its metabolites. He also was flagged for a tampering violation. His victory was also overturned to a no contest and he will be eligible to return to competition on July 30, 2024, two years from the first positive test.\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n* arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 13, 2022, at [[Pechanga Arena]] in [[San Diego]], California, United States.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/6/18/23156236/dominick-cruz-vs-marlon-vera-headlines-aug-13-ufc-event-in-san-diego|title=Dominick Cruz vs. Marlon Vera headlines Aug. 13 UFC event in San Diego|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-06-18|access-date=2022-06-18}}\n\n==Background==\nA bantamweight bout between [[Marlon Vera]] and former two-time [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] (also former [[WEC Bantamweight Championship|WEC Bantamweight Champion]]) [[Dominick Cruz]] headlined the event.\n\n[[Viviane Araújo (fighter)|Viviane Araújo]] was expected to face [[Alexa Grasso]] in a women's flyweight bout.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alexa-Grasso-Rebooked-Against-Viviane-Araujo-at-Aug-13-UFC-Fight-Night-187034|title=Alexa Grasso rebooked against Viviane Araujo at Aug. 13 UFC Fight Night|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2022-06-03|access-date=2022-06-03}} The pair was previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC 270]], but Araújo was forced to pull out from the event due to injury.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alexa-Grasso-Rebooked-Against-Viviane-Araujo-at-Aug-13-UFC-Fight-Night-187034|title=UFC 270 loses Warlley Alves, Viviane Araujo from preliminary card|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2022-01-06|access-date=2022-01-07}} In turn, Grasso pulled out due to visa issues and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-news-san-diego-espn-alexa-grasso-vs-viviane-araujo-off|title=Alexa Grasso out of UFC on ESPN 41; co-main event vs. Viviane Araujo off|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-03|access-date=2022-08-03}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Yazmin Jauregui]] and Istela Nunes was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/05/13/ufc-newcomer-yazmin-jauregui-meets-istela-nunes-august-13/|title=UFC: Newcomer Yazmin Jauregui Meets Istela Nunes on August 13|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-05-13|access-date=2022-07-17}} However, Nunes withdrew due to injury and was replaced by Iasmin Lucindo.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/nunes-lesionada-iasmin-lucindo-enfrenta-a-yazmin-jauregui-en-ufc-san-diego/|title=Nunes injured, Iasmin Lucindo faces Yazmin Jauregui at UFC San Diego|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2022-07-14|access-date=2022-07-17}} {{in lang|es}}\n\n[[Cynthia Calvillo]] and [[Nina Nunes]] were previously scheduled to meet in a women's flyweight bout at [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Fiziev]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/04/ufc-news-nina-nunes-vs-cynthia-calvillo-flyweight-bout-booked-july-9|title=Nina Nunes to make flyweight debut vs. Cynthia Calvillo at UFC Fight Night on July 9|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-04-14|access-date=2022-07-16}} In turn, Nunes fell ill on the day of that event and the bout was postponed to this date.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/9/23183509/nina-nunes-out-of-ufc-vegas-58-with-stomach-virus-hopes-to-reschedule-fight-against-cynthia-calvillo|title=Nina Nunes out of UFC Vegas 58 with ‘stomach virus,’ hopes to reschedule fight against Cynthia Calvillo|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jose Youngs|date=2022-07-09|access-date=2022-07-16}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Malcolm Gordon (fighter)|Malcolm Gordon]] and [[Allan Nascimento]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/ufc-encaminha-luta-entre-allan-puro-osso-e-malcolm-gordon-para-o-dia-13-de-agosto/|title=UFC sets up fight between Allan 'Puro Osso' and Malcolm Gordon for August 13|publisher=agfight.com|author=Diego Ribas|date=2022-06-11|access-date=2022-07-26}} {{in lang|pt}} However, Gordon pulled out in late July due to an undisclosed injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/26/23278861/malcolm-gordon-out-ufc-san-diego-fight-allan-nascimento|title=Malcolm Gordon out of UFC San Diego fight with Allan Nascimento|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-07-26|access-date=2022-07-26}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout featuring [[Aspen Ladd]] and [[Wrestling at the 2004 Summer Olympics|2004 Olympic silver medalist]] in wrestling and former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Sara McMann]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/ufc-books-aspen-ladd-sara-mcmann/|title=UFC books Aspen Ladd – Sara McMann|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2022-05-19|access-date=2022-08-01}} They were supposed to fight at [[UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker]] in June 2020, but Ladd was forced to withdraw from that event after tearing both her [[anterior cruciate ligament]] (ACL) and [[medial collateral ligament]] (MCL) in training.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/6/20/21297359/midnight-mania-aspen-ladd-sara-mcmann-ufc-espn-injury-acl|title=Midnight Mania! [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] this time.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-news-sara-mcmann-announces-aspen-ladd-out-covid-19|title=Sara McMann announces Aspen Ladd out of UFC San Diego fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-08-01|access-date=2022-08-01}} They were rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Sandhagen vs. Song]] on September 17.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/4/23293076/sara-mcmann-vs-aspen-ladd-rescheduled-for-ufc-vegas-60-on-sept-17|title=Sara McMann vs. Aspen Ladd rescheduled for UFC Vegas 60 on Sept. 17|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-08-04|access-date=2022-08-05}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Youssef Zalal]] and Cristian Quiñonez was expected to take the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/cristian-quinonez-vs-youssef-zalal-agregado-a-ufc-san-diego/|title=Cristian Quiñonez vs. Youssef Zalal added to UFC San Diego|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2022-07-27|access-date=2022-07-27}} {{in lang|es}} However, Quiñonez was pulled from the fight due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues which restricted his travel and was replaced by Da'Mon Blackshear.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-news-damon-blackshear-vs-youssef-zalal-cffc|title=CFFC bantamweight champ Da'Mon Blackshear signs with UFC, faces Youssef Zalal in San Diego|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2022-08-03|access-date=2022-08-03}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Jason Witt]] and Josh Quinlan was expected to take place one week prior at [[UFC on ESPN: Santos vs. Hill]], but it was moved to this event after Quinlan was pulled for an atypical drug finding: long-term metabolite (or M3 metabolite) of the steroid [[dehydrochloromethyltestosterone]] (DHCMT) in his urine sample. They met in a catchweight of 180 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/6/23295090/josh-quinlan-not-cleared-for-ufc-vegas-59-after-steroid-metabolite-turns-up-in-drug-test|title=Josh Quinlan not cleared for UFC Vegas 59 after steroid metabolite turns up in drug test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2022-08-06|access-date=2022-08-07}}\n\nFormer [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Women's flyweight championship|KSW Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Ariane Lipski]] and [[Priscila Cachoeira]] were previously scheduled to meet in a flyweight bout at [[UFC Fight Night: Błachowicz vs. Jacaré]], but the fight was cancelled after Cachoeira was suspended due to use of a diuretic.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/11/6/20952536/veronica-macedo-steps-in-to-fight-ariane-lipski-at-ufc-sao-paulo|title=Veronica Macedo steps in to fight Ariane Lipski at UFC Sao Paulo|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2019-11-06|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2022-08-06}} They were then expected to meet at [[UFC on ESPN: Santos vs. Hill]], but after Lipski missed weight and was not medically cleared, they were rescheduled for this event in a bantamweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-on-espn-40-news-ariane-lipski-priscila-cachoeira-bout-rescheduled-san-diego|title=Ariane Lipski not medically cleared, UFC on ESPN 40 bout vs. ESPN: Vera vs. Cruz]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Gane vs. Tuivasa]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 278: Usman vs. Edwards 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 20, 2022, at [[Vivint Arena]] in [[Salt Lake City]], [[Utah]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/11/ufc-278-announced-salt-lake-city-kamaru-usman-vs-leon-edwards-headlines/|title=UFC 278 announced for Salt Lake City, Kamaru Usman vs. Leon Edwards headlines|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-11|access-date=2022-06-11}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to [[Salt Lake City]], following [[UFC Fight Night: Rodríguez vs. Caceres]] in August 2016.{{cite web|url=https://www.abc4.com/sports/ufc-278-heads-to-salt-lake-city-with-usman-vs-edwards-2/|title=UFC 278 heads to Salt Lake City with Usman vs. Edwards 2 this summer|publisher=abc4.com|author=Vivian Chow|date=2022-06-13|access-date=2022-06-16}}\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) [[Kamaru Usman]] and [[Leon Edwards]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/6/11/23164282/ufc-278-set-for-salt-lake-city-with-kamaru-usman-vs-leon-edwards-2-headlining|title=UFC 278 set for Salt Lake City with Kamaru Usman vs. Leon Edwards 2 headlining|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2022-06-11|access-date=2022-06-11}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC on Fox: dos Anjos vs. Cowboy 2]] in December 2015 where Usman defeated Edwards by unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2015/12/ufc-on-fox-17-results-kamaru-usman-grinds-out-leon-edwards|title=UFC on FOX 17 results: Kamaru Usman grinds out Leon Edwards|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2015-12-19|access-date=2022-06-12}} \n\nA middleweight bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Luke Rockhold]] and former title challenger [[Paulo Costa]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 277]].{{cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/05/08/luke-rockhold-vs-paulo-costa-lined-ufc-277-july|title=Luke Rockhold vs. Paulo Costa lined up for UFC 277 in July|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-05-08|access-date=2022-05-26}} However, the bout was postponed to this event for unknown reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/paulo-costa-vs-luke-rockhold-moves-from-ufc-277-to-august-20-fight-booking-announcement|title=Paulo Costa vs. Luke Rockhold shifts from UFC 277 to Aug. 20 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-05-26|access-date=2022-05-26}}\n\nA flyweight bout between Victor Altamirano and Jake Hadley was scheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://mma.uno/victor-altamirano-vs-jake-hadley-agregado-a-ufc-278/|title=Victor Altamirano vs. Jake Hadley added to UFC 278|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2022-05-26|access-date=2022-05-27}} {{in lang|es}} However, after Hadley pulled out of the bout due to injury he was replaced by Daniel Lacerda.{{cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/daniel-da-silva-and-victor-altamirano-look-to-secure-first-ufc-win-at-ufc-279/|title=Daniel da Silva and Victor Altamirano look to secure first UFC win at UFC 279|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Bjorn Morfin|date=2022-06-16|access-date=2022-06-16}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Miranda Maverick]] and [[Shanna Young]] was scheduled for the event.{{cite web|date=2022-06-30|title=Miranda Maverick vs. Shanna Young booked for UFC 278 in Salt Lake City|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-278-news-miranda-maverick-vs-shanna-young-salt-lake-city|access-date=2022-08-19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} However, the bout was cancelled on the day of the weigh-ins as Young was hospitalized due to weight cut issues.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/19/23312680/miranda-maverick-vs-shanna-young-scrapped-from-ufc-278|title=Miranda Maverick vs. Shanna Young scrapped from UFC 278|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-08-19|access-date=2022-08-19}} They were rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night 214]].{{Cite web|author=Gabriel Gonzalez|date=2022-08-24|title=UFC: Maverick vs Young rebooked for November 5, Miranda Maverick signs new contract|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/08/24/ufc-maverick-vs-young-rebooked-november-05-maverick-signs-new-contract/|access-date=2022-08-24|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent The amounts do not include sponsor money, discretionary bonuses, viewership points or additional earnings. The total disclosed payout for the event was $2,542,000.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/7/23339924/ufc-278-salaries-kamaru-usman-500k-takes-home-top-disclosed-payday-in-knockout-loss|title=UFC 278 salaries: Kamaru Usman ($500k) takes home top disclosed payday in knockout loss|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2022-09-07|access-date=2022-09-07}}\n\n* '''Leon Edwards''': $350,000 (no win bonus) def. Tuivasa]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Sandhagen vs. Song]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 279: Diaz vs. Ferguson''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 10, 2022, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada|Paradise]], [[Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/2022/07/13/ufc-planeja-ter-ultimos-eventos-numerados-do-ano-para-nova-york-e-las-vegas.ghtml|title=UFC plans to have last numbered events of the year in New York and Las Vegas|publisher=ge.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2022-07-13|access-date=2022-07-19}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\n==Background==\nThe promotion originally targeted a [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between former champion [[Aljamain Sterling]] and former two-time champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]] as the main event, but opted to change plans and move it to [[UFC 280]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-279-aljamain-sterling-vs-tj-dillashaw-nate-diaz-khamzat-chimaev|title=Aljamain Sterling vs. T.J. Dillashaw no longer on UFC 279 table, but possible for UFC 280|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-07-19|access-date=2022-07-19}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-280-news-aljamain-sterling-vs-tj-dillashaw-title-fight-rescheduled-abu-dhabi|title=Aljamain Sterling vs. T.J. Dillashaw title fight rescheduled for UFC 280 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-07-20|access-date=2022-07-20}}\n\nOriginally, a welterweight bout headlining [[Khamzat Chimaev]] against [[Nate Diaz]], former lightweight title challenger and winner of [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]], was set for the event. However, Chimaev weighed in at 178.5 pounds, exceeding the non-title welterweight limit by 7.5 pounds, leading to the bout's cancellation.\n\nIn response to this, the UFC made several changes:\n\n# Nate Diaz was matched with [[Tony Ferguson]], the former interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] and winner of [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]]. Ferguson had been set to face [[Li Jingliang]] in a welterweight bout.\n# Chimaev was paired with [[Kevin Holland]], who was initially scheduled to fight [[Daniel Rodriguez (fighter)|Daniel Rodriguez]] at a catchweight of 180 pounds. Their new bout would extend to five rounds, and Chimaev wasn't fined due to this new arrangement.\n# As a result of the shuffle, Rodriguez and Jingliang were set to compete at a catchweight of 180 pounds.\n\nA trio of bouts was originally scheduled for this date, but they were eventually pushed back a week to [[UFC Fight Night: Sandhagen vs. Song]] as the promotion adjusted its schedule in June: lightweight bouts featuring Trey Ogden vs. Daniel Zellhuber and Nikolas Motta vs. Cameron VanCamp; as well as a welterweight bout between Louis Cosce and [[Trevin Giles]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-fight-night-bouts-change-shift-pay-per-view|title=With UFC 279 in the works, multiple bouts shift as September schedule tweaks|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2022-10-06|access-date=2022-09-05}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Hannah Cifers]] and Melissa Martinez was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Combate-Champ-Melissa-Martinez-to-Meet-Hannah-Cifers-at-UFC-Event-on-Sept-10-187016|title=Combate Champ Melissa Martinez to meet Hannah Cifers at UFC event on Sept. 10|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-06-01|access-date=2022-06-16}} However, Cifers pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by Elise Reed.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-279-news-hannah-cifers-out-vs-melissa-martinez|title=Hannah Cifers withdraws from UFC 279 bout vs. Melissa Martinez|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-06-19|access-date=2022-06-20}}{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/07/02/elise-reed-welcomes-melissa-martinez-ufc-ufc-279/|title=Elise Reed welcomes Melissa Martinez to the Octagon at UFC 279|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin|date=2022-07-02|access-date=2022-07-03}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Jamie Pickett]] and Denis Tiuliulin was expected to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Fiziev]], but it was moved to this card due to Tiuliulin suffering an injury during the week of the fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-june-20-26|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (June 20-26)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-06-27|access-date=2022-07-03}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/5/23195656/ufc-vegas-58-loses-jamie-pickett-vs-denis-tiuliulin-austin-lingo-vs-david-onama|title=UFC Vegas 58 loses Jamie Pickett vs. Denis Tiuliulin, Austin Lingo vs. David Onama|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2022-07-05|access-date=2022-07-06}}{{Cite bout|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-07-24|access-date=2022-09-04 }}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] and [[Jailton Almeida]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/platform/amp/2022/6/4/23153810/mma-news-jailton-almeida-renews-ufc-contract-meets-shamil-abdurakhimov-in-september|title=Jailton Almeida renews UFC contract, meets Shamil Abdurakhimov in September|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Lucas Rezende|date=2022-06-04|access-date=2022-09-01}} However, Abdurakhimov was forced to withdraw due to visa issues and was replaced by Anton Turkalj at a catchweight of 220 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/1/23331096/ufc-fight-roundup-jennifer-maia-tries-to-snap-skid-jailton-almeida-bulks-down-for-ufc-279|title=UFC Fight Roundup: Jennifer Maia tries to snap skid, Jailton Almeida bulks down for UFC 279|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2022-09-01|access-date=2022-09-01}}\n\nWhile the organization typically conducts a pre-fight press conference two days ahead of numbered events, this session was interrupted and cut short. Fighters from the final three bouts were scheduled to attend, but after an extended wait, UFC President [[Dana White]] showed up only with Holland and Rodriguez. He announced that fighters would be brought in pairs, but cancelled the press-conference after their participation instead for \"everybody's safety\".{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-279-news-dana-white-cancels-press-conference-for-everybodys-safety-after-backstage-altercations|title=Dana White cancels UFC 279 press conference 'for everybody's safety' after 'big sh*t show' backstage|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-08|access-date=2022-09-08}} He later revealed that an altercation involving four fighters and their teams took place backstage. It initiated with Chimaev and Holland, with Diaz eventually getting involved.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-279-dana-white-video-khamzat-chimaev-vs-kevin-holland-nate-diaz|title=Dana White describes scene of backstage melee at UFC 279 press conference: 'All hell broke loose'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Ken Hathaway|date=2022-09-08|access-date=2022-09-08}} The [[Nevada Athletic Commission]] (NAC) is expected to investigate the incident and potentially discipline all licensees.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/nevada-athletic-commission-to-investigate-ufc-279-press-conference-altercation-for-potential-disciplinary-action|title=Nevada Athletic Commission to investigate UFC 279 press conference altercation for potential disciplinary action|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-09-12|access-date=2022-09-12}} \n\nIn addition to Chimaev missing weight, featherweight [[Hakeem Dawodu]] and heavyweight [[Chris Barnett]] both missed weight as well. Dawodu weighed at 149.5 pounds, 3.5 pounds over the non-title featherweight limit, while Barnett weighed in at 267.5 pounds, 1.5 pounds over the non-title heavyweight limit. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight with Dawodu and Barnett each fined 30% and 20% of their purses, which went to their opponents [[Julian Erosa]] and [[Jake Collier]] respectively. A women's bantamweight bout between [[Irene Aldana]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] women's featherweight winner [[Macy Chiasson]] was also changed to a 140-pound catchweight bout, but it was not due to a weight miss.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-279-khamzat-chimaev-nate-diaz-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC 279 weigh-in results: Disastrous session leads to fight card overhaul|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-09-09|access-date=2022-09-09}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent France.]]\n\nThis event marked the organization's debut in France. After a long political battle, mixed martial arts was legalized in the country in 2020 after the [[French National Olympic and Sports Committee]] gave its approval to the French Ministry of Sport's decision to allow it under the jurisdiction of the [[French Boxing Federation]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/french-olympic-committee-says-oui-to-legalization-of-mma|title=French Olympic Committee says 'oui' to legalization of MMA|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-02-08|access-date=2022-05-19}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former interim [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Ciryl Gane]] and [[Tai Tuivasa]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/ciryl-gane-vs-tai-tuivasa-ufc-paris-sep-3/|title=Report Ciryl Gane vs. Tai Tuivasa officially announced for UFC Paris on September 3|publisher=lowkickmma.com|author=Nikhil Sharma|date=2022-06-15|access-date=2022-06-15}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) and former title challenger [[Marvin Vettori]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/5/31/23148351/robert-whittaker-vs-marvin-vettori-officially-re-booked-for-ufc-paris|title=Robert Whittaker vs. Marvin Vettori officially re-booked for UFC Paris|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-05-31|access-date=2022-05-31}} The pairing was originally scheduled for [[UFC 275]], but Whittaker withdrew due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/4/20/23033306/robert-whittaker-out-ufc-275-marvin-vettori-wants-darren-till-khamzat-chimaev-luke-rockhold|title=Robert Whittaker out of UFC 275; Marvin Vettori calls for fights against Darren Till or Khamzat Chimaev|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-04-20|access-date=2022-05-31}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Katlyn Chookagian]] was expected to face [[Manon Fiorot]] in a women's flyweight bout at the event.{{Cite Ferguson]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Dern vs. Yan]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Sandhagen vs. Song''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 210''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 68''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 60''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 17, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-news-cory-sandhagen-vs-song-yadong|title=UFC targeting Cory Sandhagen vs. Song Yadong for Sept. 17 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2022-06-17|access-date=2022-06-17}}\n\n==Background==\nA bantamweight bout between former interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Cory Sandhagen]] and [[Song Yadong]] headlined the event.\n\nA trio of bouts was originally scheduled for [[UFC 279]], but they were eventually pushed back a week to this event as the promotion adjusted its schedule in June: lightweight bouts featuring Trey Ogden vs. [[Daniel Zellhuber]] and Nikolas Motta vs. Cameron VanCamp; as well as a welterweight bout between Louis Cosce and [[Trevin Giles]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-fight-night-bouts-change-shift-pay-per-view|title=With UFC 279 in the works, multiple bouts shift as September schedule tweaks|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2022-10-06|access-date=2022-09-05}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Sijara Eubanks]] and [[Maryna Moroz]] was scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Fiziev]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/05/11/maryna-moroz-vs-sijara-eubanks-lined-ufcs-july-9-card/|title=Maryna Moroz vs. Sijara Eubanks lined up for UFC's July 9 card|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-05-11|access-date=2022-06-29}} However, the pair was moved to this event for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/17/sijara-eubanks-vs-maryna-moroz-moves-september-17-ufc-event/|title=Sijara Eubanks vs. Maryna Moroz moves to September 17 UFC event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin|date=2022-06-17|access-date=2022-06-29}} In early September, the promotion opted to rebooked Moroz against [[Jennifer Maia]] at [[UFC Fight Night 215]] after Eubanks pulled out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|author=Lucas Vera vs. Cruz]] in August 2022, but Ladd was forced to withdraw from both occasions after tearing both her [[anterior cruciate ligament]] (ACL) and [[medial collateral ligament]] (MCL) in training and [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]] respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/6/20/21297359/midnight-mania-aspen-ladd-sara-mcmann-ufc-espn-injury-acl|title=Midnight martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 15, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://talksport.com/sport/mma/649046/ufc-schedule-2022-conor-mcgregor-paddy-pimblett-israel-adesanya-francis-ngannou-usman-nunes-poirier/|title=UFC schedule 2022: Every upcoming fight including Nate Diaz's return at UFC 279 and Israel Adesanya title defence|publisher=talksport.com|author=Marc Williams|date=2022-08-28|access-date=2022-08-29}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally expected to be headlined by a middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Jared Cannonier]] and [[Sean Strickland]].{{cite web|url=https://mma.pl/jared-cannonier-vs-sean-strickland-walka-wieczoru-gali-ufc-w-pazdzierniku-obaj-przegrali-ostatnie-starcia/|title=Jared Cannonier v Sean Strickland fight for the UFC Fight Night in October. They both lost their last clashes|publisher=mma.pl|author=Mateusz Paczkowski|date=2022-08-11|access-date=2022-08-11}} {{in lang|pl}} However, the bout was scrapped after Strickland withdrew due to a finger infection.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/30/23330014/sean-strickland-out-of-headliner-with-jared-cannonier-at-ufc-event-on-oct-15|title=Sean Strickland out of headliner with Jared Cannonier at UFC event on Oct. 15|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-08-30|access-date=2022-08-30}} They instead headlined [[UFC Fight Night: Cannonier vs. Strickland]] on December 17.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-news-sean-strickland-vs-jared-cannoiner-rebooked-dec-17-fight-night-main-event|title=Jared Cannonier vs. Sean Strickland to headline UFC Fight Night event on Dec. 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-09-13|access-date=2022-09-13}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Viviane Araújo (fighter)|Viviane Araújo]] and [[Alexa Grasso]] took place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-august-8-14-2022|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-15|access-date=2022-08-15}} The pairing was previously scheduled for August 13, 2022 at [[UFC on ESPN 41]].{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alexa-Grasso-Rebooked-Against-Viviane-Araujo-at-Aug-13-UFC-Fight-Night-187034|title=Alexa Grasso rebooked against Viviane Araujo at Aug. 13 UFC Fight Night|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2022-06-03|access-date=2022-06-03}} However, Grasso pulled out due to [[visa (document)|visa]] issues which restricted her travel and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alexa-Grasso-vs-Viviane-Araujo-Removed-from-UFC-on-ESPN-41-Lineup-187590|title=Alexa Grasso vs. Viviane Araujo removed from UFC on ESPN 41 lineup|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-08-03|access-date=2022-08-15}} The pair was also previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC 270]], but Araújo pulled out from that event due to injury.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alexa-Grasso-Rebooked-Against-Viviane-Araujo-at-Aug-13-UFC-Fight-Night-187034|title=UFC 270 loses Warlley Alves, Viviane Araujo from preliminary card|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2022-01-06|access-date=2022-08-15}} They were elevated to main event status in early September after the cancellation of the original headliner.{{cite web|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/ufc/2022/ufc-vegas-62-grasso-en-araujo-vechten-het-main-event-op-15-oktober_sto8972788/story.shtml|title=UFC Vegas 62 - Everything you need to know about UFC FN: Grasso vs. Araújo|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2022-09-07|access-date=2022-09-07}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n[[Melsik Baghdasaryan]] was expected to face [[Joanderson Brito]] in a featherweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/melsik-baghdasaryan-vs-joanderson-brito-oct-15/|title=Melsik Baghdasaryan vs Joanderson Brito set for UFC Vegas 62|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2022-08-28|access-date=2022-08-28}} However, Baghdasaryan pulled out in late September due to a broken hand.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/28/23377222/broken-hand-melsik-baghdasaryan-out-ufc-vegas-62-fight-joanderson-brito|title=Broken hand knocks Melsik Baghdasaryan out of UFC Vegas 62 fight targeted for UFC Fight Night 212|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff and Nolan King|date=2022-10-09|access-date=2022-10-09}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Neil Magny]] and [[Daniel Rodriguez (fighter)|Daniel Rodriguez]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/daniel-rodriguez-vs-neil-magny-on-oct-15/|title=Daniel Rodriguez set for short notice [[UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Araújo]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Dern vs. Yan''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 211''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 69''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 61''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 1, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://talksport.com/sport/mma/649046/ufc-schedule-2022-conor-mcgregor-paddy-pimblett-israel-adesanya-francis-ngannou-usman-nunes-poirier/|title=UFC schedule 2022: Every upcoming fight including Nate Diaz's return at UFC 279 and Israel Adesanya title defence|publisher=talksport.com|author=Marc Williams|date=2022-08-28|access-date=2022-08-29}}\n\n==Background==\nUsually the UFC sells a limited number of VIP tickets for events at the [[UFC Apex]], but it was announced at the beginning of fight week that this event would be closed to the public and media. Media Day and the weigh-ins were available for media, but fight night was off-limits.{{cite web|url=https://www.sportingnews.com/us/mma/news/ufc-vegas-61-why-fans-media-wont-attendance-mackenzie-dern-yan-xiaonan/dusleumksp6k4evlxlfgnyvn|title=UFC Vegas 61: Why fans and media won't be in attendance for Mackenzie Dern vs. Yan Xiaonan|publisher=sportingnews.com|author=Daniel Yanofsky|date=2022-09-28|access-date=2022-09-28}} Though no reason for those measures was revealed, it was rumored that co-founder of [[Facebook]] and its parent company [[Meta Platforms]] (formerly Facebook, Inc.) [[Mark Zuckerberg]] booked the arena for a private event, something that UFC president [[Dana White]] vehemently denied.{{cite web|url=https://www.mirror.co.uk/sport/other-sports/mma/dana-white-mark-zuckerberg-ufc-28111397|title=Dana White denies claims concerning Mark Zuckerberg and private UFC event|publisher=mirror.co.uk|author=Donagh Corby|date=2022-09-29|access-date=2022-10-01}} Despite the denial, Zuckerberg and his wife were the only fans spotted at the Apex during the broadcast.{{cite web|url=https://www.barstoolsports.com/blog/3436326/mark-zuckerberg-and-his-wife-are-the-only-fans-at-the-ufc-apex-right-now|title=Mark Zuckerberg and his wife are the only fans at the UFC Apex right now|publisher=barstoolsports.com|author=Robbie Fox|date=2022-10-01|access-date=2022-10-01}}\n\nThe event was headlined by a women's strawweight bout between [[Mackenzie Dern]] and [[Yan Xiaonan]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/07/ufc-news-mackenzie-dern-yan-xiaonan-headline-october-1-fight-night|title=Mackenzie Dern vs. Yan Xiaonan headlines October UFC Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Mike Bohn|date=2022-07-08|access-date=2022-07-08}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Cody Garbrandt]] and [[Rani Yahya]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/ufc-rebooks-cody-garbrandt-vs-rani-yahya-for-oct-1-event|title=UFC rebooks Cody Garbrandt vs. Rani Yahya for Oct. 1 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-08-08|access-date=2022-08-08}} The pairing was previously scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Fiziev]], but Yahya withdrew due to a neck injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/6/13/23166461/rani-yahya-out-of-ufc-vegas-58-fight-with-cody-garbrandt-due-to-neck-injury|title=Rani Yahya out of UFC Vegas 58 fight with Cody Garbrandt due to neck injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2022-06-13|access-date=2022-08-08}} In turn, Yahya withdrew again in mid-September for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-mma-news-report-thrilling-ufc-co-main-event-involving-former-champion-called-off-after-star-contender-pulls-out-of-fight|title=REPORT: thrilling UFC co-main event involving former champion called off after star contender pulls 'out' of fight|publisher=essentiallysports.com|author=Afshan Kahn|date=2022-09-15|access-date=2022-09-15}} The bout was scrapped entirely when Garbrandt suffered an injury in mid-September.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/20/23364111/cody-garbrandt-pulled-from-ufc-vegas-61-due-to-injury|title=Cody Garbrandt pulled from UFC Vegas 61 due to injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2022-09-20|access-date=2022-09-20}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Vinc Pichel]] and [[Jesse Ronson]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mma.uno/vinc-pichel-vs-jesse-ronson-agregado-a-ufc-fight-night-del-1-de-octubre/|title=Vinc Pichel vs. Jesse Ronson added to UFC Fight Night on October 1|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2022-06-04|access-date=2022-06-04}} {{in lang|es}} However, Pichel withdraw due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[Joaquim Silva (fighter)|Joaquim Silva]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/18/23312222/with-vinc-pichel-out-joaquim-silva-returns-to-face-jesse-ronson-at-oct-1-ufc-event|title=With Vinc Pichel out, Joaquim Silva returns to face Jesse Ronson at Oct. 1 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2022-08-18|access-date=2022-08-18}}\n\n[[Cheyanne Vlismas]] and [[Tabatha Ricci]] were scheduled to meet in a women's strawweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/07/ufc-tabatha-ricci-face-cheyanne-vilsmas-october-1/|title=UFC: Tabatha Ricci to face Cheyanne Vlismas on October 1|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin and Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-07|access-date=2022-06-08}} Vlismas pulled out in late August for personal reasons and was replaced by former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger and inaugural [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Jessica Penne]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/08/jessica-penne-vs-tabatha-ricci-ufc-fight-night-211-cheyanne-vlismas-out-women-mma-matchup-booking|title=With Cheyanne Vlismas out, Jessica Penne steps in to face Tabatha Ricci at UFC Fight Night 211|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-08-29|access-date=2022-08-29}} However, the day before the event, the bout was scrapped as Penne withdrew due to illness.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/jessica-penne-illness-scraps-ufc-fight-night-211-bout-with-tabatha-ricci|title=Jessica Penne illness scraps UFC Fight Night 211 bout with Tabatha Ricci|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-09-30|access-date=2022-09-30}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ilir Latifi]] and [[Aleksei Oleinik]] was rescheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-august-8-22-28-2022?taid=630cca9189033000017d4d1d|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (Aug. 22-28)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-29|access-date=2022-08-29}} The pairing has been scheduled and cancelled twice before: first for [[UFC on ESPN: Blaydes vs. Daukaus]] and later at [[UFC 273]], but Latifi pulled out each time.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/3/26/22997714/ilir-latifi-out-of-aleksei-oleinik-fight-at-ufc-columbus|title=Ilir Latifi out of Aleksei Oleinik fight at UFC Columbus|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-03-26|access-date=2022-03-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/3/31/23004738/aleksei-oleinik-now-fights-jared-vanderaa-at-ufc-273-ilir-latifi-out|title=Aleksei Oleinik now fights Jared Vanderaa at UFC 273, Ilir Latifi out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2022-03-31|access-date=2022-03-31}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Chelsea Chandler]] and [[Leah Letson]] were scheduled to meet in a women's bantamweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/06/01/ufc-leah-letson-back-bantamweight-chelsea-chandler-oct-1/|title=UFC: Leah Letson back to bantamweight, welcomes Chelsea Chandler to octagon on Oct. 1|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-06-01|access-date=2022-06-02}} However, Letson pulled out in late August for personal reasons and was replaced by [[Julija Stoliarenko]], therefore changing the pairing to a catchweight of 140 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportas.lt/naujiena/462350/j-stoliarenko-apie-svajoniu-pravarde-ufc-premija-didziausius-remejus-ir-artejancia-kova|title=J. Stoliarenko: about the \"nickname\" of her dreams, the UFC bonus, the biggest sponsors and the upcoming fight|publisher=sportas.lt|author=Mantas Stankevičius|date=2022-08-30|access-date=2022-09-26}} {{in lang|lt}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Mike Davis (fighter)|Mike Davis]] and [[Uroš Medić]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-mma-news-matchup-roundup-august-8-15-21-2022|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (Aug. 15-21)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-08-22|access-date=2022-08-22}} However, Medić was removed from the bout for undisclosed reasons and replaced by Viacheslav Borshchev.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/medic-fuera-viacheslav-borshchev-enfrenta-a-mike-davis-en-ufc-vegas-61/|title=Medic out, Viacheslav Borshchev faces Mike Davis at UFC Vegas for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/08/18/jairzinho-rozenstruik-vs-chris-daukaus-set-october-1-ufc-event/|title=Jairzinho Rozenstruik vs. Chris Daukaus set for October 1 UFC web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/09/ufc-282-news-chris-daukaus-vs-jairzinho-rozenstruik|title=UFC 282 lands Chris Daukaus vs. Jairzinho Rozenstruik after bout pushed back|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2022-09-18|access-date=2022-09-18}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Maxim Grishin]] and [[Philipe Lins]] was planned for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/2022/08/26/ufc-encaminha-philipe-lins-contra-maxim-grishin-1o-de-outubro.ghtml|title=UFC books Philipe Lins against Maxim Grishin on October 1st|publisher=ge.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2022-08-26|access-date=2022-10-01}} {{in lang|pt}} Despite both men weighing in successfully, the bout was cancelled while the event was in progress due to an undisclosed medical issue.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/10/01/ufc-vegas-61-results-philipe-lins-vs-maxim-grishin/|title=UFC Vegas 61: Philipe Lins vs. Maxim Grishin
